Summary


     In this chapter we have reviewed existing
classification systems and looked at some of the
problems associated with classification. By
partitioning the San Dieguito ceramic data in
various ways. some of the reasons for the
classification problems have become evident. It
is only with some understanding about the
nature of the ceramic universe that the discrete
data sets from the site can be placed In
perspective for comparative purposes.
